# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime from user secrets.
# Users configure this via the Secrets panel in the AI Studio UI.

# AI Studio automatically injects this at runtime with the Cloud Run service URL.
# Used for self-referential links, OAuth callbacks, and API endpoints.
APP_URL="MY_APP_URL"

# Run and deploy your AI Studio app

This contains everything you need to run your app locally.

View your app in AI Studio: https://ai.studio/apps/f0bb0312-38cd-49e6-9397-71091e3fc38e

## Run Locally

**Prerequisites:**  Node.js


1. Install dependencies:
   `npm install`
2. Set the `GEMINI_API_KEY` in [.env.local](.env.local) to your Gemini API key
3. Run the app:
   `npm run dev`


==================== FILE: README_VSCODE.md ====================
# PANDUAN PENGGUNAAN PROYEK BK DI VS CODE (SMA MUHAMMADIYAH SINGAPARNA)

Proyek ini telah dikonfigurasi dan siap dijalankan secara lokal di Visual Studio Code (VS Code).

---

## 📁 Struktur File & Folder di VS Code

```text
├── public/                     # Asset statis & file unduhan
├── src/
│   ├── components/             # Komponen antarmuka (UI)
│   │   ├── HomeVisitView.tsx   # Fitur Home Visit + Upload Foto Kompresi + Modal & Cetak
│   │   ├── SiswaView.tsx       # Data Siswa (X-1 s/d XII-3) tanpa kolom NISN
│   │   ├── KonselingView.tsx   # Catatan Konseling Individu / Kelompok
│   │   ├── PelanggaranView.tsx # Catatan Pelanggaran Siswa
│   │   ├── KeterlambatanView.tsx # Rekap Keterlambatan Siswa
│   │   ├── Dashboard.tsx       # Statistik & Ringkasan Aktivitas BK
│   │   ├── AnalisisView.tsx    # Grafik & Analisis Data
│   │   ├── TindakLanjutView.tsx # Rekomendasi & Tindak Lanjut
│   │   ├── SettingsView.tsx    # Pengaturan Guru BK & Reset Catatan Aman
│   │   ├── Sidebar.tsx         # Navigasi & Tautan Google Drive
│   │   ├── BkLogo.tsx          # Logo Profesional Bimbingan dan Konseling
│   │   └── Login.tsx           # Layar Masuk Petugas BK
│   ├── lib/
│   │   ├── firebase.ts         # Koneksi Firebase Firestore
│   │   └── utils.ts            # Helper utility kelas Tailwind
│   ├── utils/
│   │   └── imageCompressor.ts  # Kompresi foto otomatis sebelum upload
│   ├── types.ts                # TypeScript interfaces (Siswa, HomeVisit dg foto, dll)
│   ├── App.tsx                 # Root component aplikasi
│   ├── main.tsx                # Entry point React
│   └── index.css               # Styling Tailwind CSS
├── index.html                  # HTML entry point
├── package.json                # Dependensi proyek (React, Vite, Lucide, Tailwind, Firebase)
├── tsconfig.json               # Konfigurasi TypeScript
├── vite.config.ts              # Konfigurasi Vite
└── firestore.rules             # Aturan keamanan database Firestore
```

---

## 🚀 Langkah Menjalankan di Visual Studio Code

### Langkah 1: Buka Folder di VS Code
1. Ekstrak file zip yang telah Anda unduh.
2. Buka aplikasi **Visual Studio Code**.
3. Klik menu **File** > **Open Folder...** (atau tekan `Ctrl + K, Ctrl + O`).
4. Pilih folder hasil ekstrak proyek ini.

### Langkah 2: Buka Terminal di VS Code
- Klik menu **Terminal** > **New Terminal** (atau tekan shortcut `Ctrl + ~` / `Ctrl + \``).

### Langkah 3: Install Dependensi (Hanya Sekali)
Di dalam terminal VS Code, jalankan perintah:
```bash
npm install
```
*(Tunggu hingga seluruh paket selesai dipasang di folder `node_modules`)*

### Langkah 4: Jalankan Server Development
Setelah install selesai, jalankan perintah:
```bash
npm run dev
```
Terminal akan menampilkan alamat lokal, biasanya:
`http://localhost:3000` atau `http://localhost:5173`

Buka alamat tersebut di Google Chrome atau browser pilihan Anda.

---

## 📸 Fitur Khusus: Dokumentasi Foto Home Visit
- Terletak pada: `src/components/HomeVisitView.tsx`
- Menggunakan modul: `src/utils/imageCompressor.ts`
- Mendukung:
  1. Upload multiple foto (JPG, PNG, WebP)
  2. Kompresi otomatis (max 1280px, kualitas 75%) sehingga hemat penyimpanan database dan cepat diakses
  3. Preview thumbnail interaktif di kartu riwayat kunjungan
  4. Lightbox layar penuh dengan navigasi slide (sebelumnya/selanjutnya) dan tombol Unduh Foto
  5. Lembar cetak resmi format A4 lengkap dengan Kop Surat SMA Muhammadiyah Singaparna, tabel data siswa, uraian masalah, tindak lanjut, dan lampiran dokumentasi foto.

---

## 👤 Guru Pembimbing
- **Jundi Jundulloh, S.Pd** (Guru Bimbingan dan Konseling SMA Muhammadiyah Singaparna)

==================== FILE: src/App.tsx ====================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  collection, 
  onSnapshot, 
  query, 
  orderBy, 
  doc, 
  getDocFromServer 
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db, OperationType, handleFirestoreError } from './lib/firebase.ts';
import { Siswa, Konseling, HomeVisit, Pelanggaran, Keterlambatan, Settings } from './types.ts';
import Login from './components/Login.tsx';
import Sidebar from './components/Sidebar.tsx';
import Dashboard from './components/Dashboard.tsx';
import SiswaView from './components/SiswaView.tsx';
import KonselingView from './components/KonselingView.tsx';
import HomeVisitView from './components/HomeVisitView.tsx';
import PelanggaranView from './components/PelanggaranView.tsx';
import KeterlambatanView from './components/KeterlambatanView.tsx';
import SettingsView from './components/SettingsView.tsx';
import AnalisisView from './components/AnalisisView.tsx';
import TindakLanjutView from './components/TindakLanjutView.tsx';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'motion/react';

type Page = 'dashboard' | 'siswa' | 'pelanggaran' | 'konseling' | 'homevisit' | 'keterlambatan' | 'pengaturan' | 'analisis' | 'tindaklanjut';

export default function App() {
  const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(false);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState<Page>('dashboard');
  
  const [siswa, setSiswa] = useState<Siswa[]>([]);
  const [konseling, setKonseling] = useState<Konseling[]>([]);
  const [homeVisit, setHomeVisit] = useState<HomeVisit[]>([]);
  const [pelanggaran, setPelanggaran] = useState<Pelanggaran[]>([]);
  const [keterlambatan, setKeterlambatan] = useState<Keterlambatan[]>([]);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<Settings>({
    guru_nama: 'Jundi Jundulloh, S.Pd'
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    async function testConnection() {
      try {
        await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'test', 'connection'));
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof Error && error.message.includes('the client is offline')) {
          console.error("Please check your Firebase configuration.");
        }
      }
    }
    testConnection();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubSiswa = onSnapshot(query(collection(db, 'siswa'), orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')), 
      (snapshot) => {
        setSiswa(snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() } as Siswa)));
      }, 
      (error) => handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, 'siswa')
    );

    const unsubKonseling = onSnapshot(query(collection(db, 'konseling'), orderBy('tanggal', 'desc')), 
      (snapshot) => {
        setKonseling(snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() } as Konseling)));
      }, 
      (error) => handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, 'konseling')
    );

    const unsubHomeVisit = onSnapshot(query(collection(db, 'homevisit'), orderBy('tanggal', 'desc')), 
      (snapshot) => {
        setHomeVisit(snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() } as HomeVisit)));
      }, 
      (error) => handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, 'homevisit')
    );

    const unsubPelanggaran = onSnapshot(query(collection(db, 'pelanggaran'), orderBy('tanggal', 'desc')), 
      (snapshot) => {
        setPelanggaran(snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() } as Pelanggaran)));
      }, 
      (error) => handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, 'pelanggaran')
    );

    const unsubKeterlambatan = onSnapshot(query(collection(db, 'keterlambatan'), orderBy('tanggal', 'desc')), 
      (snapshot) => {
        setKeterlambatan(snapshot.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() } as Keterlambatan)));
      }, 
      (error) => handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, 'keterlambatan')
    );

    const unsubSettings = onSnapshot(doc(db, 'settings', 'config'), 
      (snapshot) => {
        if (snapshot.exists()) {
          const data = snapshot.data();
          setSettings({
            guru_nama: data.guru_nama || data.guru1_nama || 'Jundi Jundulloh, S.Pd',
            guru1_nama: data.guru1_nama,
            guru2_nama: data.guru2_nama
          });
        }
      }, 
      (error) => handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, 'settings/config')
    );

    return () => {
      unsubSiswa();
      unsubKonseling();
      unsubHomeVisit();
      unsubPelanggaran();
      unsubKeterlambatan();
      unsubSettings();
    };
  }, []);

  if (!isLoggedIn) {
    return <Login onLogin={() => setIsLoggedIn(true)} />;
  }

  const renderContent = () => {
    switch (currentPage) {
      case 'dashboard':
        return <Dashboard siswa={siswa} konseling={konseling} homeVisit={homeVisit} pelanggaran={pelanggaran} keterlambatan={keterlambatan} />;
      case 'siswa':
        return <SiswaView siswa={siswa} />;
      case 'konseling':
        return <KonselingView konseling={konseling} siswa={siswa} settings={settings} />;
      case 'homevisit':
        return <HomeVisitView homeVisit={homeVisit} siswa={siswa} settings={settings} />;
      case 'pelanggaran':
        return <PelanggaranView pelanggaran={pelanggaran} siswa={siswa} />;
      case 'keterlambatan':
        return <KeterlambatanView keterlambatan={keterlambatan} siswa={siswa} />;
      case 'tindaklanjut':
        return <TindakLanjutView konseling={konseling} homeVisit={homeVisit} siswa={siswa} />;
      case 'pengaturan':
        return <SettingsView 
          settings={settings} 
          siswa={siswa}
          konseling={konseling}
          homeVisit={homeVisit}
          pelanggaran={pelanggaran}
          keterlambatan={keterlambatan}
          allDataCount={siswa.length + konseling.length + homeVisit.length + pelanggaran.length + keterlambatan.length} 
        />;
      case 'analisis':
        return <AnalisisView siswa={siswa} konseling={konseling} homeVisit={homeVisit} pelanggaran={pelanggaran} keterlambatan={keterlambatan} settings={settings} />;
      default:
        return <Dashboard siswa={siswa} konseling={konseling} homeVisit={homeVisit} pelanggaran={pelanggaran} keterlambatan={keterlambatan} />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden font-sans">
      <Sidebar currentPage={currentPage} onNavigate={setCurrentPage} onLogout={() => setIsLoggedIn(false)} />
      
      <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col relative overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="flex-1 p-4 md:p-8">
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            <motion.div
              key={currentPage}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
            >
              {renderContent()}
            </motion.div>
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
        
        <footer className="bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 py-4 text-center text-xs">
          © {new Date().getFullYear()} Jundi Jundulloh, S.Pd - BK SMA MUDASPA
        </footer>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

==================== FILE: src/lib/firebase.ts ====================
import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth } from 'firebase/auth';
import { getFirestore } from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
export const db = getFirestore(app, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
export const auth = getAuth(app);

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  }
}

export function handleFirestoreError(error: unknown, operationType: OperationType, path: string | null) {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo: auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map(provider => ({
        providerId: provider.providerId,
        email: provider.email,
      })) || []
    },
    operationType,
    path
  }
  console.error('Firestore Error: ', JSON.stringify(errInfo));
  throw new Error(JSON.stringify(errInfo));
}

}


==================== FILE: src/utils/imageCompressor.ts ====================
/**
 * Client-side image compressor for Firestore storage.
 * Compresses images to compact JPEG base64 strings so records remain well below Firestore's 1MB limit.
 */
export async function compressImage(
  file: File, 
  maxWidth = 1024, 
  maxHeight = 1024, 
  quality = 0.72
): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Gagal membaca file gambar'));
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Gagal memuat gambar'));
      img.onload = () => {
        let width = img.width;
        let height = img.height;

        // Calculate aspect-ratio-preserving dimensions
        if (width > height) {
          if (width > maxWidth) {
            height = Math.round((height * maxWidth) / width);
            width = maxWidth;
          }
        } else {
          if (height > maxHeight) {
            width = Math.round((width * maxHeight) / height);
            height = maxHeight;
          }
        }

        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;

        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          reject(new Error('Gagal membuat context canvas 2D'));
          return;
        }

        // Draw and compress to jpeg
        ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

        const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality);
        resolve(dataUrl);
      };

      if (typeof e.target?.result === 'string') {
        img.src = e.target.result;
      } else {
        reject(new Error('Format file tidak didukung'));
      }
    };

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

/**
 * Calculates human-readable base64 size in KB
 */
export function getBase64SizeKb(dataUrl: string): number {
  const head = 'data:image/jpeg;base64,';
  const len = dataUrl.startsWith('data:') ? dataUrl.length - (dataUrl.indexOf(',') + 1) : dataUrl.length;
  return Math.round((len * 3) / 4 / 1024);
}

import React from 'react';

interface AnalisisViewProps {

export default function AnalisisView({ siswa, konseling, homeVisit, pelanggaran, keterlambatan, settings }: AnalisisViewProps) {
  const activeGuru = settings.guru_nama || settings.guru1_nama || 'Jundi Jundulloh, S.Pd';

  // 1. Aktivitas Bimbingan Guru BK (Konseling vs Home Visit)
  const aktivitasGuruData = [
    { name: 'Konseling', value: konseling.length, color: '#3b82f6' },
    { name: 'Home Visit', value: homeVisit.length, color: '#10b981' },
  ].filter(d => d.value > 0);

  // 2. Analisis Layanan Konseling
  const layananData = [
    { name: 'Pribadi', value: konseling.filter(k => k.layanan === 'Pribadi').length, color: '#3b82f6' },
    { name: 'Sosial', value: konseling.filter(k => k.layanan === 'Sosial').length, color: '#10b981' },
    { name: 'Akademik', value: konseling.filter(k => k.layanan === 'Akademik').length, color: '#f59e0b' },
    { name: 'Karir', value: konseling.filter(k => k.layanan === 'Karir').length, color: '#8b5cf6' },
  ].filter(d => d.value > 0);

  // 3. Jenis Pelanggaran Terbanyak
  const pelanggaranMap = new Map<string, number>();
  pelanggaran.forEach(p => {
    pelanggaranMap.set(p.jenis, (pelanggaranMap.get(p.jenis) || 0) + 1);
  });

  const topJenisPelanggaran = Array.from(pelanggaranMap.entries())
    .map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value)
    .slice(0, 5);

  // 4. Siswa Teraktif (Aggregated)
  const activityMap = new Map<string, {
    pribadi: number,
    karir: number,
    akademik: number,
    sosial: number,
    homevisit: number,
    pelanggaran: number,
    terlambat: number,
    total: number
  }>();

  const getSiswaActivity = (id: string) => {
    if (!activityMap.has(id)) {
      activityMap.set(id, { pribadi: 0, karir: 0, akademik: 0, sosial: 0, homevisit: 0, pelanggaran: 0, terlambat: 0, total: 0 });
    }
    return activityMap.get(id)!;
  };

  konseling.forEach(k => {
    const act = getSiswaActivity(k.siswaId);
    if (k.layanan === 'Pribadi') act.pribadi++;
    else if (k.layanan === 'Karir') act.karir++;
    else if (k.layanan === 'Akademik') act.akademik++;
    else if (k.layanan === 'Sosial') act.sosial++;
    act.total++;
  });

  homeVisit.forEach(hv => {
    const act = getSiswaActivity(hv.siswaId);
    act.homevisit++;
    act.total++;
  });

  pelanggaran.forEach(p => {
    const act = getSiswaActivity(p.siswaId);
    act.pelanggaran++;
    act.total++;
  });

  keterlambatan.forEach(k => {
    const act = getSiswaActivity(k.siswaId);
    act.terlambat++;
    act.total++;
  });

  const topSiswaActivity = siswa
    .filter(s => activityMap.has(s.id))
    .map(s => ({ ...s, stats: activityMap.get(s.id)! }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.stats.total - a.stats.total)
    .slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Analisis & Statistik Internal</h1>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm mt-1">Laporan komprehensif efektivitas bimbingan dan peta kondisi siswa.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
        {/* Aktivitas Guru BK */}
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200">
           <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800">Aktivitas Bimbingan</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-600 font-bold">{activeGuru}</p>
            </div>
            <PieChartIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="h-64">
            {aktivitasGuruData.length > 0 ? (
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <PieChart>
                  <Pie
                    data={aktivitasGuruData}
                    cx="50%"
                    cy="50%"
                    innerRadius={60}
                    outerRadius={80}
                    paddingAngle={5}
                    dataKey="value"
                    label={({ name, value }) => `${name}: ${value}`}
                  >
                    {aktivitasGuruData.map((entry, index) => (
                      <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.color} />
                    ))}
                  </Pie>
                  <Tooltip />
                  <Legend verticalAlign="bottom" height={36}/>
                </PieChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 text-sm italic">
                Belum ada aktivitas bimbingan tercatat
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Distribusi Layanan */}
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
            <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800">Distribusi Bidang Layanan</h2>
            <PieChartIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="h-64">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <PieChart>
                <Pie
                  data={layananData}
                  cx="50%"
                  cy="50%"
                  innerRadius={60}
                  outerRadius={80}
                  paddingAngle={5}
                  dataKey="value"
                  label
                >
                  {layananData.map((entry, index) => (
                    <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.color} />
                  ))}
                </Pie>
                <Tooltip />
                <Legend verticalAlign="bottom" height={36}/>
              </PieChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Ranking Pelanggaran */}
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800 mb-6">Peta Pelanggaran Tertinggi</h2>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {topJenisPelanggaran.map((item, idx) => {
              const percentage = Math.round((item.value / pelanggaran.length) * 100);
              return (
                <div key={item.name} className="space-y-1.5">
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                    <span className="text-slate-600 line-clamp-1">{idx + 1}. {item.name}</span>
                    <span className="text-rose-600">{item.value} Insiden</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full bg-slate-100 rounded-full h-2.5 overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="bg-rose-500 h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-1000" style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
            {topJenisPelanggaran.length === 0 && <p className="text-center py-10 text-slate-400 italic">Belum ada data</p>}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Siswa Teraktif Bimbingan */}
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200">
          <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800 mb-6">Siswa Teraktif Bimbingan</h2>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {topSiswaActivity.map((s, idx) => (
              <div key={s.id} className="p-5 bg-slate-50/50 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="bg-indigo-600 w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-white shadow-sm text-xs">
                      {idx + 1}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm leading-tight">{s.nama}</p>
                      <p className="text-slate-400 text-[10px] items-center flex gap-1 font-medium"><Users className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> {s.kelas}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <span className="text-indigo-600 font-black text-xl">{s.stats.total}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest ml-1">Total</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Pribadi</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-blue-500">{s.stats.pribadi}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Karir</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-indigo-500">{s.stats.karir}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Akademik</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-amber-500">{s.stats.akademik}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Sosial</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-emerald-500">{s.stats.sosial}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Home Visit</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-pink-500">{s.stats.homevisit}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Pelanggaran</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-rose-500">{s.stats.pelanggaran}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[9px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase leading-none mb-1">Terlambat</p>
                    <p className="text-xs font-black text-orange-500">{s.stats.terlambat}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {topSiswaActivity.length === 0 && <p className="text-center py-10 text-slate-400 italic">Belum ada data</p>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== FILE: src/components/BkLogo.tsx ====================
import React from 'react';

interface BkLogoProps extends React.SVGProps<SVGSVGElement> {
  className?: string;
  size?: number | string;
}

/**
 * Logo Resmi & Profesional Bimbingan dan Konseling (BK) MUDASPA
 * 
 * Filosofi Logo:
 * 1. Dua Tangan Merangkul / Sayap Pelindung (Nurturing Hands): Simbol kepedulian, empati, pendampingan, dan ruang aman (safe space) bagi siswa.
 * 2. Sosok Insan Berkembang (Flourishing Student): Simbol pertumbuhan pribadi, kemandirian, dan aktualisasi potensi siswa.
 * 3. Bintang Penuntun Masa Depan (Guiding Star): Arah cita-cita, pencerahan moral, solusi, dan masa depan yang cerah.
 * 4. Perisai Harmoni & Lingkaran Persaudaraan: Melindungi integritas, kerahasiaan konseling (asas kerahasiaan BK), dan persaudaraan Muhammadiyah.
 */
export default function BkLogo({ className = "w-10 h-10", size, ...props }: BkLogoProps) {
  const uniqueId = React.useId().replace(/:/g, '');
  const gradPrimary = `bk-grad-primary-${uniqueId}`;
  const gradAccent = `bk-grad-accent-${uniqueId}`;
  const gradGold = `bk-grad-gold-${uniqueId}`;
  const gradGlow = `bk-grad-glow-${uniqueId}`;

  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 120 120"
      fill="none"
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
      className={className}
      width={size}
      height={size}
      aria-label="Logo Bimbingan dan Konseling"
      {...props}
    >
      <defs>
        {/* Gradien Utama: Deep Indigo ke Royal Violet & Cyan */}
        <linearGradient id={gradPrimary} x1="10" y1="10" x2="110" y2="110" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#4338CA" />
          <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#6366F1" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#38BDF8" />
        </linearGradient>

        {/* Gradien Aksen: Emerald to Cyan (Pertumbuhan & Ketenangan) */}
        <linearGradient id={gradAccent} x1="20" y1="90" x2="100" y2="30" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#10B981" />
          <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#06B6D4" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#60A5FA" />
        </linearGradient>

        {/* Gradien Emas: Pencerahan, Harapan & Kebijaksanaan */}
        <linearGradient id={gradGold} x1="60" y1="10" x2="60" y2="45" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#FDE047" />
          <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#F59E0B" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#D97706" />
        </linearGradient>

        {/* Subtle radial aura */}
        <radialGradient id={gradGlow} cx="60" cy="55" r="45" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#818CF8" stopOpacity="0.25" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#4F46E5" stopOpacity="0" />
        </radialGradient>

        <filter id={`shadow-${uniqueId}`} x="-10%" y="-10%" width="120%" height="130%" filterUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="3" stdDeviation="3" floodColor="#1E1B4B" floodOpacity="0.25" />
        </filter>
      </defs>

      {/* Latar Belakang Perisai Membulat Lembut Modern */}
      <rect
        x="6"
        y="6"
        width="108"
        height="108"
        rx="28"
        fill={`url(#${gradPrimary})`}
      />

      {/* Subtle Inner Highlight Ring */}
      <rect
        x="8"
        y="8"
        width="104"
        height="104"
        rx="26"
        stroke="white"
        strokeOpacity="0.2"
        strokeWidth="1.5"
      />

      {/* Cahaya Lingkaran Aura Dalam */}
      <circle cx="60" cy="58" r="38" fill={`url(#${gradGlow})`} />

      {/* Grup Lambang BK Utama */}
      <g filter={`url(#shadow-${uniqueId})`}>
        {/* Tangan Peduli Kiri (Nurturing Hand & Wing Curve) */}
        <path
          d="M 28 76 C 28 92 48 98 60 98 C 50 90 42 80 40 68 C 38 56 46 45 48 40 C 42 45 34 52 30 62 C 28.5 66 28 71 28 76 Z"
          fill="white"
          fillOpacity="0.95"
        />

        {/* Tangan Peduli Kanan (Nurturing Hand & Wing Curve) */}
        <path
          d="M 92 76 C 92 92 72 98 60 98 C 70 90 78 80 80 68 C 82 56 74 45 72 40 C 78 45 86 52 90 62 C 91.5 66 92 71 92 76 Z"
          fill="white"
          fillOpacity="0.95"
        />

        {/* Lengkungan Penopang Bawah (Pondasi Kehangatan & Kasih Sayang) */}
        <path
          d="M 36 78 C 44 88 54 92 60 92 C 66 92 76 88 84 78 C 76 85 68 87 60 87 C 52 87 44 85 36 78 Z"
          fill={`url(#${gradAccent})`}
        />

        {/* Sosok Figur Siswa yang Berkembang & Bahagia di Tengah */}
        {/* Kepala Siswa */}
        <circle cx="60" cy="46" r="6.5" fill="white" />

        {/* Tubuh & Tangan Terangkat Siswa Menyambut Masa Depan */}
        <path
          d="M 60 55 C 53 55 49 61 47 70 C 51 68 56 67 60 67 C 64 67 69 68 73 70 C 71 61 67 55 60 55 Z"
          fill="white"
        />

        {/* Daun Tunas Potensi / Harapan di Bahu */}
        <path
          d="M 60 64 C 55 60 51 62 48 65 C 52 64 56 65 60 67 Z"
          fill={`url(#${gradAccent})`}
        />
        <path
          d="M 60 64 C 65 60 69 62 72 65 C 68 64 64 65 60 67 Z"
          fill={`url(#${gradAccent})`}
        />

        {/* Bintang Penuntun & Pencerahan Masa Depan (Guiding Star of BK) di Puncak */}
        {/* Cahaya Inti Bintang 8 Sudut */}
        <path
          d="M 60 17 L 62.2 26 L 69 23 L 64.5 28.5 L 72 32 L 63.8 33.5 L 67 40 L 60.5 36 L 60 41 L 59.5 36 L 53 40 L 56.2 33.5 L 48 32 L 55.5 28.5 L 51 23 L 57.8 26 Z"
          fill={`url(#${gradGold})`}
        />

        {/* Inti Pusat Bintang */}
        <circle cx="60" cy="30" r="3" fill="#FFFBEB" />
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
}


==================== FILE: src/components/Dashboard.tsx ====================
import React from 'react';
import { 
  Users, 
  MessageCircle, 
  Home, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  AlertCircle,
  TrendingUp,
  UserCheck
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Siswa, Konseling, HomeVisit, Pelanggaran, Keterlambatan } from '../types.ts';
import { 
  BarChart, 
  Bar, 
  XAxis, 
  YAxis, 
  CartesianGrid, 
  Tooltip, 
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Cell
} from 'recharts';

interface DashboardProps {
  siswa: Siswa[];
  konseling: Konseling[];
  homeVisit: HomeVisit[];
  pelanggaran: Pelanggaran[];
  keterlambatan: Keterlambatan[];
}

export default function Dashboard({ siswa, konseling, homeVisit, pelanggaran, keterlambatan }: DashboardProps) {
  const stats = [
    { label: 'Total Siswa', value: siswa.length, icon: Users, color: 'indigo' },
    { label: 'Konseling', value: konseling.length, icon: MessageCircle, color: 'blue' },
    { label: 'Home Visit', value: homeVisit.length, icon: Home, color: 'emerald' },
    { label: 'Pelanggaran', value: pelanggaran.length, icon: AlertTriangle, color: 'rose' },
    { label: 'Terlambat', value: keterlambatan.length, icon: AlertCircle, color: 'orange' },
  ];

  const chartData = [
    { name: 'Konseling', value: konseling.length, color: '#3b82f6' },
    { name: 'Home Visit', value: homeVisit.length, color: '#10b981' },
    { name: 'Pelanggaran', value: pelanggaran.length, color: '#f43f5e' },
    { name: 'Terlambat', value: keterlambatan.length, color: '#f97316' },
  ];

  // Hitung keterlambatan per siswa
  const keterlambatanMap = new Map<string, number>();
  keterlambatan.forEach(k => {
    keterlambatanMap.set(k.siswaId, (keterlambatanMap.get(k.siswaId) || 0) + 1);
  });

  const topSiswaTerlambat = siswa
    .filter(s => keterlambatanMap.has(s.id))
    .map(s => ({ ...s, count: keterlambatanMap.get(s.id) || 0 }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
    .slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Overview Dashboard</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm mt-1">Selamat datang di sistem manajemen BK SMA MUDASPA.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200">
          <UserCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600" />
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{siswa.length} Siswa Terdaftar</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
        {stats.map((stat) => (
          <div key={stat.label} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-100 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow group">
            <div className={`p-3 rounded-xl inline-flex mb-4 transition-colors bg-${stat.color}-50 text-${stat.color}-600 group-hover:bg-${stat.color}-600 group-hover:text-white`}>
              <stat.icon className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{stat.label}</p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1">{stat.value}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
        {/* Aktivitas Chart */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-100">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
            <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-900">Aktivitas Bimbingan</h2>
            <TrendingUp className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="h-80 w-full">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <BarChart data={chartData}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#f1f5f9" />
                <XAxis dataKey="name" axisLine={false} tickLine={false} tick={{ fill: '#64748b', fontSize: 12 }} />
                <YAxis axisLine={false} tickLine={false} tick={{ fill: '#64748b', fontSize: 12 }} />
                <Tooltip 
                  cursor={{ fill: '#f8fafc' }}
                  contentStyle={{ borderRadius: '12px', border: 'none', boxShadow: '0 10px 15px -3px rgb(0 0 0 / 0.1)' }}
                />
                <Bar dataKey="value" radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]} barSize={40}>
                  {chartData.map((entry, index) => (
                    <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.color} />
                  ))}
                </Bar>
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Top Siswa Terlambat */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-100">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-8">
            <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-slate-900">Top Keterlambatan</h2>
            <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-slate-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {topSiswaTerlambat.length > 0 ? (
              topSiswaTerlambat.map((s) => (
                <div key={s.id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-4 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="bg-white w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-indigo-600 shadow-xs border border-slate-100">
                      {s.nama.charAt(0)}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-sm">{s.nama}</p>
                      <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs">{s.kelas}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="text-xl font-black text-rose-600">{s.count}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Kali</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            ) : (
              <div className="text-center py-12 flex flex-col items-center">
                <div className="bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-full mb-4">
                   <AlertCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-300" />
                </div>
                <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm italic">Belum ada data keterlambatan</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== FILE: src/components/HomeVisitView.tsx ====================
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  Plus, 
  Search, 
  Edit2, 
  Trash2, 
  X,
  MapPin,
  Calendar,
  User,
  Heart,
  Camera,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Upload,
  Download,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Printer,
  FileText,
  AlertCircle
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Siswa, HomeVisit, Settings } from '../types.ts';
import { 
  collection, 
  addDoc, 
  updateDoc, 
  deleteDoc, 
  doc, 
  serverTimestamp 
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db, OperationType, handleFirestoreError } from '../lib/firebase.ts';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { id as idID } from 'date-fns/locale';
import { compressImage, getBase64SizeKb } from '../utils/imageCompressor.ts';
import BkLogo from './BkLogo.tsx';

interface HomeVisitViewProps {
  homeVisit: HomeVisit[];
  siswa: Siswa[];
  settings: Settings;
}

export default function HomeVisitView({ homeVisit, siswa, settings }: HomeVisitViewProps) {
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [editingHV, setEditingHV] = useState<HomeVisit | null>(null);

  // Form State
  const activeGuru = settings.guru_nama || settings.guru1_nama || 'Jundi Jundulloh, S.Pd';
  const [siswaId, setSiswaId] = useState('');
  const [tanggal, setTanggal] = useState(format(new Date(), 'yyyy-MM-dd'));
  const [layanan, setLayanan] = useState('');
  const [guru, setGuru] = useState(activeGuru);
  const [keterangan, setKeterangan] = useState('');
  const [tindaklanjut, setTindaklanjut] = useState('');
  const [fotoList, setFotoList] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isCompressing, setIsCompressing] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);
  const [deleteId, setDeleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Lightbox Modal State
  const [lightboxData, setLightboxData] = useState<{
    photos: string[];
    index: number;
    title: string;
    date: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Print Report Modal State
  const [printHV, setPrintHV] = useState<HomeVisit | null>(null);

  const resetForm = () => {
    setSiswaId('');
    setTanggal(format(new Date(), 'yyyy-MM-dd'));
    setLayanan('');
    setGuru(activeGuru);
    setKeterangan('');
    setTindaklanjut('');
    setFotoList([]);
    setEditingHV(null);
    setShowForm(false);
    if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
  };

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = e.target.files;
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

    if (fotoList.length + files.length > 5) {
      alert('Maksimal 5 foto dokumentasi per kunjungan untuk menjaga efisiensi penyimpanan.');
      return;
    }

    setIsCompressing(true);
    try {
      const newCompressedPhotos: string[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
        const file = files[i];
        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
          continue;
        }
        const compressed = await compressImage(file, 1024, 1024, 0.72);
        newCompressedPhotos.push(compressed);
      }
      setFotoList(prev => [...prev, ...newCompressedPhotos]);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error compressing image:', err);
      alert('Terjadi kesalahan saat memproses gambar.');
    } finally {
      setIsCompressing(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveFoto = (index: number) => {
    setFotoList(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  };

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!siswaId || !tanggal || !layanan || !guru || !keterangan) {
      alert('Semua field wajib diisi!');
      return;
    }

    setIsDeleting(true);
    try {
      if (editingHV) {
        const docRef = doc(db, 'homevisit', editingHV.id);
        await updateDoc(docRef, {
          siswaId,
          tanggal,
          layanan,
          guru,
          keterangan,
          tindaklanjut,
          foto: fotoList,
          updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
        });
      } else {
        await addDoc(collection(db, 'homevisit'), {
          siswaId,
          tanggal,
          layanan,
          guru,
          keterangan,
          tindaklanjut,
          foto: fotoList,
          createdAt: serverTimestamp()
        });
      }
      resetForm();
    } catch (error) {
      handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.WRITE, 'homevisit');
    } finally {
      setIsDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleEdit = (hv: HomeVisit) => {
    setEditingHV(hv);
    setSiswaId(hv.siswaId);
    setTanggal(hv.tanggal);
    setLayanan(hv.layanan);
    setGuru(hv.guru);
    setKeterangan(hv.keterangan);
    setTindaklanjut(hv.tindaklanjut || '');
    setFotoList(hv.foto || []);
    setShowForm(true);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!deleteId) return;
    setIsDeleting(true);
    try {
      await deleteDoc(doc(db, 'homevisit', deleteId));
      setDeleteId(null);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error deleting homevisit:", error);
      alert("Gagal menghapus data: " + (error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
    } finally {
      setIsDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  const [selectedKelas, setSelectedKelas] = useState<string>('Semua');
  const classes = Array.from(new Set(siswa.map(s => s.kelas))).sort();

  const filteredHV = homeVisit.filter(hv => {
    const targetSiswa = siswa.find(s => s.id === hv.siswaId);
    if (!targetSiswa) return false;
    
    const matchesSearch = targetSiswa.nama.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) || 
                          hv.layanan.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
                          hv.guru.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());
    
    const matchesKelas = selectedKelas === 'Semua' || targetSiswa.kelas === selectedKelas;

    return matchesSearch && matchesKelas;
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3">
            Home Visit
            <span className="text-xs bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider">
              Dokumentasi Foto
            </span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-sm mt-1">
            Kunjungan rumah untuk pemantauan perkembangan siswa disertai arsip bukti dokumentasi foto.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 flex-wrap">
          <a
            href="/homevisit-photo-update.zip"
            download="homevisit-photo-update.zip"
            className="bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition-all flex items-center gap-2 border border-slate-200 shadow-xs"
            title="Unduh file kode sumber fitur Home Visit untuk dimasukkan ke VS Code"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" /> 
            <span>File VS Code (.ZIP)</span>
          </a>
          <button 
            onClick={() => { resetForm(); setShowForm(true); }}
            className="bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition-all flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" /> Tambah Kunjungan
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Form Modal / Container */}
      {showForm && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 md:p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-emerald-100 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-teal-500 to-emerald-600" />
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-bold text-xl text-slate-800">
                {editingHV ? 'Edit Catatan Home Visit' : 'Catat Home Visit Baru'}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Lengkapi informasi kunjungan dan unggah foto dokumentasi rumah / pertemuan orang tua.</p>
            </div>
            <button onClick={resetForm} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 p-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors">
              <X className="w-6 h-6" />
            </button>
          </div>
          
          <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Pilih Siswa *</label>
              <select 
                value={siswaId} onChange={(e) => setSiswaId(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all font-medium text-slate-800"
              >
                <option value="">-- Pilih Siswa --</option>
                {siswa.map(s => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.nama} ({s.kelas})</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Tanggal Kunjungan *</label>
              <input 
                type="date"
                value={tanggal} onChange={(e) => setTanggal(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all font-medium"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Bidang Bimbingan *</label>
              <select 
                value={layanan} onChange={(e) => setLayanan(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all font-medium text-slate-800"
              >
                <option value="">-- Pilih Bidang Bimbingan --</option>
                <option>Pribadi</option>
                <option>Sosial</option>
                <option>Akademik</option>
                <option>Karir</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Guru Pelaksana</label>
              <input 
                type="text"
                readOnly
                value={guru}
                className="w-full p-3 bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold text-slate-700 cursor-not-allowed"
              />
            </div>
            
            <div className="md:col-span-2 lg:col-span-2 space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Hasil Kunjungan / Evaluasi *</label>
              <textarea 
                value={keterangan} onChange={(e) => setKeterangan(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Catatan hasil percakapan dengan orang tua/wali, kondisi lingkungan siswa, kendala yang dihadapi..." 
                className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all font-medium min-h-24"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 lg:col-span-3 space-y-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider ml-1">Rencana Tindak Lanjut / Kesimpulan</label>
              <textarea 
                value={tindaklanjut} onChange={(e) => setTindaklanjut(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Komitmen bersama orang tua, bimbingan lanjutan di sekolah, dsb..." 
                className="w-full p-3.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all font-medium min-h-20"
              />
            </div>

            {/* DOKUMENTASI FOTO UPLOAD SECTION */}
            <div className="md:col-span-2 lg:col-span-3 bg-slate-50/75 p-5 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-emerald-300">
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Camera className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800">Dokumentasi Foto Kunjungan Rumah</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Unggah foto kunjungan bersama siswa/orang tua (maks. 5 foto, otomatis dioptimalkan).</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                  {fotoList.length} / 5 Foto Terpasang
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Upload Action Bar */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <input 
                  type="file" 
                  ref={fileInputRef}
                  accept="image/*" 
                  multiple
                  onChange={handleFileUpload}
                  className="hidden" 
                  id="foto-homevisit-upload"
                  disabled={isCompressing || fotoList.length >= 5}
                />
                <label 
                  htmlFor="foto-homevisit-upload"
                  className={`cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition-all ${
                    fotoList.length >= 5 
                      ? 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-400 cursor-not-allowed'
                      : 'bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white shadow-md shadow-emerald-200 hover:shadow-lg active:scale-95'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
                  {isCompressing ? 'Sedang Memproses Foto...' : 'Pilih / Ambil Foto'}
                </label>

                <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  Mendukung JPG, PNG, WEBP (otomatis dikompresi agar hemat memori)
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Photos Preview Grid */}
              {fotoList.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-5 gap-3">
                  {fotoList.map((foto, idx) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="relative group rounded-xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-white shadow-sm bg-slate-200 aspect-video">
                      <img 
                        src={foto} 
                        alt={`Dokumentasi ${idx + 1}`} 
                        className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300" 
                      />
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setLightboxData({
                            photos: fotoList,
                            index: idx,
                            title: 'Preview Foto Dokumentasi',
                            date: tanggal
                          })}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-white/90 text-slate-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-white"
                          title="Lihat Penuh"
                        >
                          <ImageIcon className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveFoto(idx)}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-rose-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-700 shadow-md"
                          title="Hapus Foto"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                      <div className="absolute bottom-1 left-1 bg-black/60 text-white text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono">
                        #{idx + 1} • {getBase64SizeKb(foto)}KB
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            
            <div className="md:col-span-2 lg:col-span-3 flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
              <button 
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                className="px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-all text-sm"
              >
                Batal
              </button>
              <button 
                type="submit"
                disabled={isDeleting || isCompressing}
                className="bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg shadow-emerald-100 transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 text-sm flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                {isDeleting ? 'Menyimpan Data...' : editingHV ? 'Simpan Perubahan' : 'Simpan Data Home Visit'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid of Results */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xs border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 w-full md:w-auto flex-1">
            <div className="relative flex-1 md:max-w-md">
              <Search className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
              <input 
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Cari nama siswa atau guru..." 
                className="w-full pl-11 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all text-sm font-medium"
              />
            </div>
            <select 
              value={selectedKelas}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedKelas(e.target.value)}
              className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 transition-all text-sm font-bold text-slate-600 outline-none"
            >
              <option value="Semua">Semua Kelas</option>
              {classes.map(c => <option key={c} value={c}>Kelas {c}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">{filteredHV.length} Kunjungan Selesai</div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          {filteredHV.map((hv) => {
            const targetSiswa = siswa.find(s => s.id === hv.siswaId);
            if (!targetSiswa) return null;
            
            const visitDate = new Date(hv.tanggal);
            const photos = hv.foto || [];

            return (
              <div key={hv.id} className="bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-emerald-200 rounded-2xl shadow-xs hover:shadow-md transition-all group overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                <div className="p-5 flex-1 space-y-4">
                  {/* Top Header Card */}
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div className="bg-emerald-50 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-100">
                        <MapPin className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-800 text-base">{targetSiswa.nama}</h3>
                        <p className="text-emerald-700 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{targetSiswa.kelas}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-right">
                      <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
                        {format(visitDate, 'MMMM yyyy', { locale: idID })}
                      </p>
                      <p className="font-bold text-slate-700 text-xs flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-500" />
                        {format(visitDate, 'dd-MM-yyyy', { locale: idID })}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Badges */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                      {hv.layanan}
                    </span>
                    <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                      <User className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" /> {hv.guru}
                    </span>
                    {photos.length > 0 && (
                      <span className="bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Camera className="w-3 h-3" /> {photos.length} Foto Dokumentasi
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Keterangan */}
                  <div className="space-y-1 bg-slate-50/70 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Heart className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-500" /> Hasil Evaluasi / Kunjungan:
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-slate-700 text-xs leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">{hv.keterangan}</p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Tindak Lanjut */}
                  {hv.tindaklanjut && (
                    <div className="space-y-1 bg-emerald-50/50 p-3.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-100">
                      <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-700 uppercase tracking-widest">
                        Kesimpulan & Tindak Lanjut:
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-emerald-900 text-xs font-medium italic leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line">
                        {hv.tindaklanjut}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* DOKUMENTASI FOTO THUMBNAILS IN CARD */}
                  {photos.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <ImageIcon className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" /> Bukti Dokumentasi ({photos.length})
                        </span>
                        <button 
                          onClick={() => setLightboxData({
                            photos,
                            index: 0,
                            title: `Dokumentasi Home Visit - ${targetSiswa.nama}`,
                            date: hv.tanggal
                          })}
                          className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-800 transition-colors"
                        >
                          Lihat Semua
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                        {photos.map((foto, pIdx) => (
                          <div 
                            key={pIdx}
                            onClick={() => setLightboxData({
                              photos,
                              index: pIdx,
                              title: `Dokumentasi Home Visit - ${targetSiswa.nama}`,
                              date: hv.tanggal
                            })}
                            className="group/img relative rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 aspect-video cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition-all"
                          >
                            <img 
                              src={foto} 
                              alt={`Dokumentasi ${targetSiswa.nama} ${pIdx + 1}`} 
                              className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover/img:scale-110 transition-transform duration-300"
                              loading="lazy"
                            />
                            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/30 opacity-0 group-hover/img:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex items-center justify-center">
                              <ImageIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-white drop-shadow" />
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                      <span className="italic flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Camera className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-300" /> Belum ada foto dokumentasi
                      </span>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => handleEdit(hv)}
                        className="text-emerald-600 font-bold hover:underline"
                      >
                        + Tambah Foto
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Card Actions Bottom */}
                <div className="bg-slate-50/80 px-4 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-100">
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setPrintHV(hv)}
                    className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 hover:text-emerald-700 flex items-center gap-1.5 py-1 px-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white transition-all"
                    title="Cetak Laporan Home Visit"
                  >
                    <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Cetak Laporan
                  </button>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <button 
                      onClick={() => handleEdit(hv)} 
                      className="p-1.5 text-indigo-500 hover:text-indigo-700 hover:bg-white rounded-lg transition-all" 
                      title="Edit Data & Foto"
                    >
                      <Edit2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                    <button 
                      onClick={() => setDeleteId(hv.id)} 
                      className="p-1.5 text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-white rounded-lg transition-all" 
                      title="Hapus Kunjungan"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {filteredHV.length === 0 && (
            <div className="col-span-full py-20 text-center text-slate-400 italic">
              Tidak ada data home visit
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LIGHTBOX / PHOTO VIEWER MODAL */}
      {lightboxData && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-md animate-fade-in">
          <div className="relative w-full max-w-4xl bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-slate-800 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="p-4 px-6 bg-slate-900/90 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between text-white">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-slate-100">{lightboxData.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                  <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400" /> {lightboxData.date} • Foto ke {lightboxData.index + 1} dari {lightboxData.photos.length}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <a 
                  href={lightboxData.photos[lightboxData.index]} 
                  download={`Dokumentasi-HomeVisit-${lightboxData.index + 1}.jpg`}
                  className="p-2 text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-xl transition-all"
                  title="Unduh Foto Ini"
                >
                  <Download className="w-5 h-5" />
                </a>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setLightboxData(null)}
                  className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-xl transition-all"
                >
                  <X className="w-6 h-6" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Active Image Stage */}
            <div className="relative flex-1 bg-black flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-hidden min-h-[300px] md:min-h-[450px]">
              <img 
                src={lightboxData.photos[lightboxData.index]} 
                alt="Dokumentasi Full View" 
                className="max-h-[65vh] max-w-full object-contain rounded-lg shadow-2xl select-none"
              />

              {/* Prev & Next arrows if multiple */}
              {lightboxData.photos.length > 1 && (
                <>
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setLightboxData(prev => prev ? {
                      ...prev,
                      index: (prev.index - 1 + prev.photos.length) % prev.photos.length
                    } : null)}
                    className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-3 bg-slate-900/70 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-full backdrop-blur-sm transition-all"
                    title="Foto Sebelumnya"
                  >
                    <ChevronLeft className="w-6 h-6" />
                  </button>
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setLightboxData(prev => prev ? {
                      ...prev,
                      index: (prev.index + 1) % prev.photos.length
                    } : null)}
                    className="absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 p-3 bg-slate-900/70 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white rounded-full backdrop-blur-sm transition-all"
                    title="Foto Selanjutnya"
                  >
                    <ChevronRight className="w-6 h-6" />
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Bottom thumbnail strip */}
            {lightboxData.photos.length > 1 && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-slate-900 border-t border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center gap-3 overflow-x-auto">
                {lightboxData.photos.map((p, idx) => (
                  <button 
                    key={idx}
                    onClick={() => setLightboxData(prev => prev ? { ...prev, index: idx } : null)}
                    className={`relative w-16 h-12 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border-2 transition-all shrink-0 ${
                      lightboxData.index === idx ? 'border-emerald-500 scale-105' : 'border-transparent opacity-60 hover:opacity-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <img src={p} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* PRINTABLE REPORT MODAL */}
      {printHV && (() => {
        const student = siswa.find(s => s.id === printHV.siswaId);
        const photos = printHV.foto || [];
        return (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm">
            <div className="relative bg-white w-full max-w-3xl rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
              {/* Toolbar */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between print:hidden">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
                  <span className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800">Format Laporan Home Visit</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => window.print()}
                    className="bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-2 shadow-md shadow-emerald-100 transition-all"
                  >
                    <Printer className="w-4 h-4" /> Cetak Sekarang
                  </button>
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => setPrintHV(null)}
                    className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-200 transition-all"
                  >
                    <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Printable Body */}
              <div className="p-8 overflow-y-auto space-y-6 text-slate-800 font-sans print:p-0 print:m-0">
                {/* School Header */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b-2 border-slate-800 pb-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <BkLogo size="md" />
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="font-black text-lg text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-tight">SMA MUHAMMADIYAH SINGAPARNA</h2>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700 tracking-wide uppercase">Layanan Bimbingan dan Konseling (BK)</p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">Jl. Panyingkiran No. 37, Singaparna, Kab. Tasikmalaya</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">Lampiran BK-03</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Report Title */}
                <div className="text-center space-y-1">
                  <h3 className="text-base font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-900 underline">
                    LAPORAN PELAKSANAAN HOME VISIT (KUNJUNGAN RUMAH)
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">Nomor: HV/{format(new Date(printHV.tanggal), 'yyyy/MM')}/{printHV.id.substring(0, 5)}</p>
                </div>

                {/* Identity Table */}
                <div className="bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs space-y-2">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">Nama Siswa:</span>
                    <span className="col-span-2 font-bold text-slate-900">{student?.nama || '-'}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">Kelas / Jenis Kelamin:</span>
                    <span className="col-span-2 font-medium text-slate-900">{student?.kelas} / {student?.jk}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">Tanggal Kunjungan:</span>
                    <span className="col-span-2 font-medium text-slate-900">{format(new Date(printHV.tanggal), 'EEEE, dd MMMM yyyy', { locale: idID })}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">Bidang Bimbingan:</span>
                    <span className="col-span-2 font-medium text-slate-900">{printHV.layanan}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">Guru BK Pelaksana:</span>
                    <span className="col-span-2 font-bold text-slate-900">{printHV.guru}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Contents */}
                <div className="space-y-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1 border-b border-slate-200 pb-1">
                      A. Deskripsi Kondisi & Hasil Kunjungan:
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line p-2 bg-slate-50/50 rounded-lg">
                      {printHV.keterangan}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1 border-b border-slate-200 pb-1">
                      B. Kesimpulan & Rencana Tindak Lanjut:
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line p-2 bg-slate-50/50 rounded-lg italic">
                      {printHV.tindaklanjut || 'Belum ada tindak lanjut khusus yang dicatat.'}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* LAMPIRAN DOKUMENTASI FOTO */}
                  <div>
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span>C. Lampiran Dokumentasi Foto:</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 normal-case">{photos.length} Foto Terlampir</span>
                    </h4>
                    {photos.length > 0 ? (
                      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2">
                        {photos.map((foto, idx) => (
                          <div key={idx} className="border border-slate-300 rounded-lg p-1 bg-white">
                            <img 
                              src={foto} 
                              alt={`Dokumentasi ${idx + 1}`} 
                              className="w-full h-44 object-cover rounded" 
                            />
                            <p className="text-[9px] text-center text-slate-500 mt-1 font-mono">
                              Dokumentasi #{idx + 1} - Kunjungan {student?.nama}
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-slate-400 italic text-[11px] p-3 text-center bg-slate-50 rounded-lg">
                        Tidak ada dokumentasi foto yang dilampirkan.
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Signatures */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-8 pt-8 text-center text-xs">
                  <div className="space-y-16">
                    <p className="font-medium text-slate-600">Orang Tua / Wali Siswa,</p>
                    <p className="font-bold border-b border-slate-400 pb-1 w-44 mx-auto">( ............................................ )</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-16">
                    <p className="font-medium text-slate-600">Guru Bimbingan Konseling,</p>
                    <p className="font-bold border-b border-slate-400 pb-1 w-44 mx-auto text-slate-900">{printHV.guru}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })()}

      {/* Delete Confirmation Modal */}
      {deleteId && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => !isDeleting && setDeleteId(null)} />
          <div className="relative bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200">
            <div className="p-6 bg-rose-50 border-b border-rose-100">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-rose-600">
